# FINAL whole-branch review package — Фаза 3 VibeFPS (Лобби/очки/покупки/двери/сохранение)
Base: b7ad7c5 (HEAD). Рабочее дерево НЕ закоммичено (политика проекта: коммитит владелец).

 func _toggle_mouse_capture() -> void:
 	if Input.mouse_mode == Input.MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED:
 		Input.set_mouse_mode(Input.MOUSE_MODE_VISIBLE)
 	else:
 		Input.set_mouse_mode(Input.MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED)

+## ╨Я╤Г╨▒╨╗╨╕╤З╨╜╤Л╨╣: ╨╛╨║╤А╨╡╤Б╤В╨╜╨╛╤Б╤В╤П╨╝ ╨╗╨╛╨▒╨▒╨╕ (╨┐╨╛╨┤╤Б╨▓╨╡╤В╨║╨░ ╤В╨╛╤З╨║╨╕) ╨╛╨╜ ╨┐╨╛╨╜╨░╨┤╨╛╨▒╨╕╤В╤Б╤П, ╨░ ╤В╨╡╤Б╤В╤Г тАФ

+func nearest_interactable() -> Node:
+	var nearest: Node = null
+	var nearest_dist := _INTERACT_RANGE
+	for node in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("interactable"):
+		# ╨в╨╛╤З╨║╨░ ╨▓╨╖╨░╨╕╨╝╨╛╨┤╨╡╨╣╤Б╤В╨▓╨╕╤П тАФ Area3D, ╨░ ╨╖╨╜╨░╤З╨╕╤В Node3D; ╤Г╨╖╨╡╨╗ ╨▒╨╡╨╖ ╨╝╨╕╤А╨╛╨▓╨╛╨╣
+		# ╨┐╨╛╨╖╨╕╤Ж╨╕╨╕ ╨▓ ╨▓╤Л╨▒╨╛╤А╨║╤Г ╨╜╨╡ ╨┐╨╛╨┐╨░╨┤╤С╤В. ╨Я╤А╨╛╨▓╨╡╤А╨║╨░ ╨╜╨░ ╨┤╨╡╤А╨╡╨▓╨╛ тАФ ╤З╤В╨╛╨▒╤Л ╤Г╨╖╨╡╨╗,
+		# ╨╛╨╢╨╕╨┤╨░╤О╤Й╨╕╨╣ ╤Г╨┤╨░╨╗╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П, ╨╜╨╡ ╨┐╨╛╨┐╨░╨╗ ╨▓ ╨╛╤В╨▓╨╡╤В.
+		if not (node is Node3D):
+			continue
+		if not node.is_inside_tree():
+			continue
+		var dist := global_position.distance_to((node as Node3D).global_position)
+		if dist > _INTERACT_RANGE:
+			continue
+		if nearest == null or dist < nearest_dist:
+			nearest = node
+			nearest_dist = dist
+	return nearest
+
+
+## ╨Т╤Л╨┐╨╛╨╗╨╜╨╕╤В╤М ╨▓╨╖╨░╨╕╨╝╨╛╨┤╨╡╨╣╤Б╤В╨▓╨╕╨╡ ╤Б ╨▒╨╗╨╕╨╢╨░╨╣╤И╨╡╨╣ ╤В╨╛╤З╨║╨╛╨╣. false, ╨╡╤Б╨╗╨╕ ╤В╨╛╤З╨║╨╕ ╨╜╨╡╤В, ╤Г ╨╜╨╡╤С
+## ╨╜╨╡╤В ╨┐╤А╨╛╤В╨╛╨║╨╛╨╗╨░ interact ╨╕╨╗╨╕ ╤Б╨░╨╝ ╨▓╤Л╨╖╨╛╨▓ ╨▓╨╡╤А╨╜╤Г╨╗ ╨╛╤В╨║╨░╨╖.
+func _try_interact() -> bool:
+	var target := nearest_interactable()
+	if target == null or not target.has_method("interact"):

+	return bool(target.call("interact", self))

 func _respawn(restore_health: bool = false) -> void:
 	global_transform = _spawn_transform
 	velocity = Vector3.ZERO
 	_jump_queued = false
 	_camera.rotation.x = 0.0

 		return

 	health_changed.emit(_health, max_health)

+## ╨Я╤А╨╛╨▒╨╜╤Л╨╣ GameState: ╤В╨╛╤В ╨╢╨╡ ╨║╨╗╨░╤Б╤Б, ╤З╤В╨╛ ╤Г ╨░╨▓╤В╨╛╨╖╨░╨│╤А╤Г╨╖╨║╨╕ (Task 3), ╨╜╨╛ ╤Б

+## `scene_changer` (GameState ╨╖╨╛╨▓╤С╤В ╨╡╨│╨╛ ╤В╨╛╨╗╤М╨║╨╛ ╤Б ╨┐╤Г╤В╤С╨╝ ╤Б╤Ж╨╡╨╜╤Л): ┬л╤Б╨╝╨╡╤А╤В╤М ╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╨╕╤В
+## ╨╗╨╛╨▒╨▒╨╕┬╗ ╨╕ ┬л╤Б╨╝╨╡╤А╤В╤М ╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╨╕╤В ╨╗╨╛╨▒╨▒╨╕ ╨Я╨Ю ╨Я╨а╨Ш╨з╨Ш╨Э╨Х ╤Б╨╝╨╡╤А╤В╨╕┬╗ тАФ ╤А╨░╨╖╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╤Г╤В╨▓╨╡╤А╨╢╨┤╨╡╨╜╨╕╤П, ╨╕
+## ╨▓╤В╨╛╤А╨╛╨╡ ╨▓╨╕╨┤╨╜╨╛ ╤В╨╛╨╗╤М╨║╨╛ ╨╕╨╖╨╜╤Г╤В╤А╨╕ ╤Б╨░╨╝╨╛╨│╨╛ ╨▓╤Л╨╖╨╛╨▓╨░.
+class _RecordingGameState extends GameState:
+	var lobby_reasons: PackedStringArray = []
+
+	func return_to_lobby(reason: String) -> void:
+		lobby_reasons.append(reason)
+		super.return_to_lobby(reason)
+
+
+## ╨Я╤А╨╛╨▒╨╜╤Л╨╣ GameState, ╨┐╨╛╨║╨░ ╨╛╨╜ ╤Б╤В╨╛╨╕╤В ╨▓ /root. null ╨╛╨╖╨╜╨░╤З╨░╨╡╤В ┬л╨▓ root ╨╡╨│╨╛ ╨╜╨╡╤В┬╗ тАФ
+## ╤А╨╛╨▓╨╜╨╛ ╤В╨╛ ╤Б╨╛╤Б╤В╨╛╤П╨╜╨╕╨╡, ╨║╨╛╤В╨╛╤А╨╛╨│╨╛ ╤В╤А╨╡╨▒╤Г╤О╤В fallback-╤Б╨╡╨║╤Ж╨╕╤П ╤Б╨╝╨╡╤А╤В╨╕ ╨╕ ╨▓╤Б╨╡ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨▓╨╡╤А╨║╨╕

+var _probe_game_state: GameState = null
+## ╨Я╤Г╤В╨╕, ╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤И╨╡╨╜╨╜╤Л╨╡ ╤Г ╨┐╨╛╨┤╨╝╨╡╨╜╤С╨╜╨╜╨╛╨│╨╛ scene_changer, ╨▓ ╨┐╨╛╤А╤П╨┤╨║╨╡ ╨▓╤Л╨╖╨╛╨▓╨░.
+var _lobby_changes: PackedStringArray = []
+
+
+## ╨Ч╨░╨┐╨╕╤Б╤Л╨▓╨░╤О╤Й╨░╤П ╨╖╨░╨│╨╗╤Г╤И╨║╨░ ╤Б╨╝╨╡╨╜╤Л ╤Б╤Ж╨╡╨╜╤Л: ╤Д╨╕╨║╤Б╨╕╤А╤Г╨╡╤В ╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б ╨╕ ╨╜╨╕╤З╨╡╨│╨╛ ╨╜╨╡ ╨╝╨╡╨╜╤П╨╡╤В.
+func _fake_scene_changer(path: String) -> void:
+	_lobby_changes.append(path)
+
+
+## ╨б╤В╨░╨▓╨╕╤В ╨┐╤А╨╛╨▒╨╜╤Л╨╣ GameState ╨▓ /root. ╨Ш╨╝╤П "GameState" тАФ ╤З╨░╤Б╤В╤М ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╤А╨░╨║╤В╨░: ╨╕
+## player.gd, ╨╕ enemy_contact.gd ╨╜╨░╤Е╨╛╨┤╤П╤В ╨╡╨│╨╛ ╨┐╨╛ ╨┐╤Г╤В╨╕ /root/GameState, ╨░ ╨╜╨╡ ╨┐╨╛
+## ╤Б╤Б╤Л╨╗╨║╨╡, ╨┐╨╛╤Н╤В╨╛╨╝╤Г ╨┐╨╡╤А╨╡╨╕╨╝╨╡╨╜╨╛╨▓╨░╨╜╨╕╨╡ ╤Г╨╖╨╗╨░ ╤Б╨╗╨╛╨╝╨░╨╗╨╛ ╨▒╤Л ╨╛╨▒╨╡ ╨┐╤А╨╛╨▓╨╡╤А╨║╨╕.
+func _install_probe_game_state() -> GameState:
+	var gs := _RecordingGameState.new()
+	gs.name = "GameState"
+	gs.scene_changer = _fake_scene_changer
+	root.add_child(gs)
+	_probe_game_state = gs
+	return gs
+
+
+## ╨г╨▒╨╕╤А╨░╨╡╤В ╨┐╤А╨╛╨▒╨╜╤Л╨╣ GameState ╨╕╨╖ /root. ╨Ю╨▒╤П╨╖╨░╤В╨╡╨╗╤М╨╜╨╛ ╨Ф╨Ю ╤Б╨╡╨║╤Ж╨╕╨╣, ╨║╨╛╤В╨╛╤А╤Л╨╝ ╨╜╤Г╨╢╨╡╨╜
+## fallback: ╨╢╨╕╨▓╨╛╨╣ /root/GameState ╨╝╨╡╨╜╤П╨╡╤В ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╤А╨░╨║╤В ╤Б╨╝╨╡╤А╤В╨╕.
+func _remove_probe_game_state() -> void:
+	if _probe_game_state != null and is_instance_valid(_probe_game_state):
+		_probe_game_state.queue_free()
+	_probe_game_state = null
+	await physics_frame
+
+
+## PackedStringArray vs Array ╨┐╨╛ ╤Б╨╛╨┤╨╡╤А╨╢╨╕╨╝╨╛╨╝╤Г: Godot ╨╜╨╡ ╤Б╤З╨╕╤В╨░╨╡╤В ╨┤╨▓╨░ ╤А╨░╨╖╨╜╤Л╤Е
+## ╨║╨╛╨╜╤В╨╡╨╣╨╜╨╡╤А╨░ ╤А╨░╨▓╨╜╤Л╨╝╨╕, ╨┐╨╛╤Н╤В╨╛╨╝╤Г `packed == ["death"]` ╨▒╤Л╨╗╨╛ ╨▒╤Л false ╨┤╨░╨╢╨╡ ╨┐╤А╨╕
+## ╤Б╨╛╨▓╨┐╨░╨┤╨╡╨╜╨╕╨╕ ╤Б╨╛╨┤╨╡╤А╨╢╨╕╨╝╨╛╨│╨╛.
+func _packed_strings_eq(packed: PackedStringArray, expected: Array) -> bool:
+	if packed.size() != expected.size():
+		return false
+	for i in packed.size():
+		if packed[i] != expected[i]:
+			return false
+	return true
+
+

+## The engine registers GameState/SaveSystem as project autoloads
+## (project.godot, Task 6) and installs nodes of those names under /root even in
+## --script mode. The whole harness relies on /root/GameState being ABSENT
+## (fallback death contract) or on a probe it installs itself; the autoload would
+## shadow the probe (Godot renames the later node when the name is taken) and
+## hand player.gd the real change_scene_to_file, which would swap in lobby.tscn
+## and add a second group-"player" node. Drop them first, so /root/GameState
+## means what the harness says it means. Immediate free, not queue_free: the
+## name must be free before the probe is added.
+func _drop_autoloads() -> void:
+	for autoload_name in ["GameState", "SaveSystem"]:
+		var node := root.get_node_or_null(autoload_name)
+		if node != null:
+			root.remove_child(node)
+			node.free()
+
+
 func _initialize() -> void:
 	print("=== enemies ===")
+	_drop_autoloads()
 
 	print("-- %s must load as an EnemyData" % _CONTACT_PATH)
 	var res: Resource = load(_CONTACT_PATH)
 	if res == null:
 		_bail("cannot load %s" % _CONTACT_PATH)
 		return
 	var script_res := res.get_script() as Script
 	_check(res is EnemyData, "contact.tres is an EnemyData",
 		"(script=%s)" % ("none" if script_res == null else script_res.resource_path))
 	var data := res as EnemyData

 		"a zero spawn interval is rejected")

 	await _test_contact_enemy()

 	if _bailed:
 		# _bail() ╤Г╨╢╨╡ ╨╜╨░╨┐╨╡╤З╨░╤В╨░╨╗ ╤Б╨▓╨╛╨┤╨║╤Г ╨╕ ╨╖╨░╨┐╤А╨╛╤Б╨╕╨╗ quit(1). ╨Т╤В╨╛╤А╨╛╨╣ quit() ╨╖╨┤╨╡╤Б╤М
 		# ╨┐╨╡╤А╨╡╨╖╨░╨┐╨╕╤Б╨░╨╗ ╨▒╤Л ╨║╨╛╨┤ ╨▓╨╛╨╖╨▓╤А╨░╤В╨░ ╨╜╤Г╨╗╤С╨╝, ╨╕ FATAL ╨┐╤А╨╛╤З╨╕╤В╨░╨╗╨╕ ╨▒╤Л ╨║╨░╨║ PASS.

@@ -73,11 +74,80 @@ func _test_sky_background(world: Node3D) -> void:
 	if we == null or we.environment == null:

 		return
 	var env := we.environment
 	_check(env.background_mode == Environment.BG_SKY, "background_mode is BG_SKY (2)",

 	_check(env.sky != null, "environment has a Sky resource")
 	if env.sky != null:

\ No newline at end of file
+		_check(env.sky.sky_material != null, "sky has a sky_material")
+
+
+## Lobby assembly check: the home scene the player lands in must carry the floor
+## to stand on, the player, the two interaction points and the three test-range
+## targets. Loaded and instantiated exactly as the game loads it -- a harness
+## that rebuilt the nodes would stay green on a lobby.tscn with nothing in it.
+## Run last, and leave main.tscn's section above untouched.
+func _test_lobby() -> void:
+	print("-- lobby: scene assembly")
+	var packed: PackedScene = load("res://lobby.tscn")
+	_check(packed != null, "res://lobby.tscn loads")
+	if packed == null:
+		return
+
+	var lobby: Node3D = packed.instantiate()
+	_check(lobby != null, "lobby.tscn instantiates to a node")
+	if lobby == null:
+		return
+	root.add_child(lobby)
+	# _ready() sets layers, groups and hurtbox zones; they are only readable once
+	# the nodes have gone through a frame in the tree.
+	await physics_frame
+
+	_check(lobby is Node3D, "lobby root is a Node3D")
+	_check(lobby.get_node_or_null("Player") != null, "Player is instantiated in the lobby")
+
+	var floor := lobby.get_node_or_null("Floor") as StaticBody3D
+	_check(floor != null, "Floor StaticBody3D exists")
+	if floor != null:
+		_check(int(floor.collision_layer) == 1,
+			"the lobby floor is on layer 1 (world)",
+			"(got %d)" % int(floor.collision_layer))
+
+	var shop := lobby.get_node_or_null("ShopPoint")
+	_check(shop != null, "ShopPoint exists")
+	if shop != null:
+		_check(shop.is_in_group("interactable"),
+			"ShopPoint is in the interactable group")
+		_check(int(shop.collision_layer) == 1 << 5,
+			"ShopPoint is on layer 6 (interactable)",
+			"(got %d)" % int(shop.collision_layer))
+
+	var door := lobby.get_node_or_null("DoorPoint")
+	_check(door != null, "DoorPoint exists")
+	if door != null:
+		_check(door.is_in_group("interactable"),
+			"DoorPoint is in the interactable group")
+		_check(int(door.collision_layer) == 1 << 5,
+			"DoorPoint is on layer 6 (interactable)",
+			"(got %d)" % int(door.collision_layer))
+
+	var target_names := ["TargetNear", "TargetMid", "TargetFar"]
+	var targets: Array[Node] = []
+	for target_name in target_names:
+		var target := lobby.get_node_or_null(target_name)
+		_check(target != null, "%s exists" % target_name)
+		if target != null:
+			targets.append(target)
+	_check(targets.size() == 3, "three targets stand in the lobby",
+		"(got %d)" % targets.size())
+	for target in targets:
+		var hurtbox := target.get_node_or_null("Hurtbox") as Hurtbox
+		_check(hurtbox != null, "%s carries a Hurtbox" % target.name)
+		if hurtbox != null:
+			_check(hurtbox.zone == 0,
+				"%s hurtbox is zone 0 (enemy_hurtbox)" % target.name,
+				"(got %d)" % hurtbox.zone)
+
+	lobby.queue_free()
\ No newline at end of file
diff --git a/tests/weapon.gd b/tests/weapon.gd
index 9578f8e..d092ef6 100644
--- a/tests/weapon.gd
+++ b/tests/weapon.gd
@@ -90,37 +90,37 @@ const _EXPECTED_LIFETIME := 4.0
 const _WAIT_CAP_SECONDS := 15.0
 ## How far a measured timer may overshoot its nominal value. The timers are real
 ## seconds drained by per-frame deltas, so the answer overshoots by at most one
 ## frame -- and still nowhere near enough to hide a doubled interval, a reset
 ## timer or a reload that never ends.
 const _TIMER_TOLERANCE := 0.35
 ## Float slack on the short side of a timer, so accumulated delta rounding can
 ## never fake a timer that ended early.
 const _TIMER_EPSILON := 0.05
 ## How many physics ticks the flight is watched for after the aim source is
-## turned and moved. At the shipped 120 Hz and 40 m/s that is half a second and
-## 20 m of flight -- long enough for a bend of the parent to be unmistakable,
-## short enough to stay inside the 3 s projectile lifetime.
+## turned and moved. At the shipped 120 Hz and 70 m/s that is half a second and
+## 35 m of flight -- long enough for a bend of the parent to be unmistakable,
+## short enough to stay inside the 4 s projectile lifetime.
 const _TRAJECTORY_TICKS := 60
 ## How far off its own line the projectile may drift, in metres. The bug this
 ## catches moves a shot sideways by METRES (a parent yaw of 45 deg rotates the
-## whole subtree around the pivot), while float32 noise over 20 m is about
-## 2e-6 m. Centimetres separate the two with four orders of magnitude to spare.
+## whole subtree around the pivot), while float32 noise over 35 m is about
+## 3e-6 m. Centimetres separate the two with four orders of magnitude to spare.
 const _TRAJECTORY_DRIFT_TOLERANCE := 0.05
 ## How far along the line the projectile must actually have travelled for the
 ## drift measurement above to mean anything. A projectile that never moved
 ## reports a drift of exactly zero and would satisfy the tolerance without
 ## having flown at all.
 const _TRAJECTORY_MIN_TRAVEL := 2.0
 ## How many seconds of real frames the startup check watches a weapon that
-## entered the tree with the button held. Twice the 0.5 s rate limit, so a
-## pistol that fires on level alone would have spent four rounds.
+## entered the tree with the button held. Two and a half times the 0.4 s rate
+## limit, so a pistol that fires on level alone would have spent three rounds.
 const _NO_SHOT_WATCH_SECONDS := 1
 
 var _failures: PackedStringArray = []
 var _checks := 0
 var _data: WeaponData
 ## One PackedScene for every pistol in this harness, standing in for the
 ## projectile.tscn that Task 5 will build on disk.
 var _projectile_scene: PackedScene
 
 
@@ -617,21 +617,21 @@ func _test_shot_keeps_its_line_when_the_aim_source_moves() -> void:
 		# Distance from the line, not from the origin: the shot is SUPPOSED to
 		# travel along `line`, so only the sideways component is drift.
 		worst = maxf(worst, (offset - line * offset.dot(line)).length())
 		travelled = maxf(travelled, offset.dot(line))
 	print("    worst sideways drift %.4f m, forward travel %.3f m over %d physics ticks" % [
 		worst, travelled, _TRAJECTORY_TICKS])
 	# Precondition, and it is load-bearing. Drift of zero is what a CORRECT shot
 	# looks like -- and also what a projectile that never moved looks like: the
 	# loop would sample one position forever and pass the tolerance below for
 	# the wrong reason. So the flight is proved first, along the line the bullet
-	# is supposed to follow. Half a second at 40 m/s is ~20 m; the threshold is
+	# is supposed to follow. Half a second at 70 m/s is ~35 m; the threshold is
 	# 2.0 m, far below that and far above the sub-millimetre distance a frozen
 	# bullet accumulates.
 	_check(travelled > _TRAJECTORY_MIN_TRAVEL,
 		"precondition: the projectile really flew, so a drift of zero means a straight shot",
 		"(travelled %.3f m along the line, want > %.1f m)" % [travelled, _TRAJECTORY_MIN_TRAVEL])
 	_check(worst <= _TRAJECTORY_DRIFT_TOLERANCE,
 		"the shot flew straight: moving the aim source's parent did not bend it",
 		"(worst sideways drift %.4f m, tolerance %.3f m)" % [worst, _TRAJECTORY_DRIFT_TOLERANCE])
 	_check(is_instance_valid(bullet), "and the projectile was still alive at the end of the flight")
 	world.free()
@@ -738,21 +738,21 @@ func _test_no_shot_while_the_button_is_down_on_entry() -> void:
 	pistol.projectile_scene = _projectile_scene
 	var log := _watch(pistol)
 	world.add_child(pistol)
 	for i in 8:
 		await process_frame
 		if pistol.is_node_ready():
 			break
 	_check(pistol.gate_mouse_mode == Input.MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED,
 		"precondition: the input gate is open, so only the button can hold the shot back")
 
-	# A full second of real frames with the button down -- longer than the 0.5 s
+	# A full second of real frames with the button down -- longer than the 0.4 s
 	# rate limit, so a pistol that fires on level alone would spend three rounds.
 	for i in _NO_SHOT_WATCH_SECONDS * 60:
 		await process_frame
 	_check(log.fired == 0,
 		"a weapon that entered the tree with fire held does not shoot",
 		"(got %d shots, magazine %d of %d)" % [log.fired, pistol.current_mag(), _EXPECTED_MAGAZINE])
 	_check(pistol.current_mag() == _EXPECTED_MAGAZINE,
 		"and it kept every round", "(got %d)" % pistol.current_mag())
 
 	# The player's answer to "nothing happened": let go and press again.

--- FILE: scripts/save_system.gd ---
class_name SaveSystem
extends Node

## Autosave of the game state to a JSON file under user://.
##
## Phase 3, Track B: this script will be registered as a project autoload of the
## form *res://scripts/save_system.gd in project.godot (a later task); until
## then the tests instantiate it manually against an isolated path.

@export var save_path: String = "user://save.json"


## Serializes the payload to JSON and writes it to save_path.
## Returns false when the file cannot be opened or the write reports an error,
## so callers never mistake a failed save for a successful one.
func save(payload: Dictionary) -> bool:
	var file := FileAccess.open(save_path, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if file == null:
		return false
	file.store_string(JSON.stringify(payload))
	var err := file.get_error()
	file.close()
	return err == OK


## Reads save_path back as a Dictionary. A missing or unreadable file, a file
## whose content is not valid JSON, or valid JSON that is not an object all
## come back as {} вЂ” a load must never crash the caller over a bad save file.
func load_state() -> Dictionary:
	var file := FileAccess.open(save_path, FileAccess.READ)
	if file == null:
		return {}
	var parsed: Variant = JSON.parse_string(file.get_as_text())
	return parsed if parsed is Dictionary else {}


## Deletes the save file. A missing file is a non-event.
func clear_save() -> void:
	DirAccess.remove_absolute(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(save_path))


## True when a save file currently exists at save_path.
func has_save() -> bool:
	return FileAccess.file_exists(save_path)

class_name GameState
extends Node

## Р“Р»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµСЃСЃР°: РѕС‡РєРё, РєСѓРїР»РµРЅРЅРѕРµ Рё СЌРєРёРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕРµ РѕСЂСѓР¶РёРµ,
## РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚С‹Рµ Р»РѕРєР°С†РёРё. Р–РёРІС‘С‚ РєР°Рє Р°РІС‚РѕР·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° /root/GameState (РїСЂРѕРІРѕРґРєР° вЂ” Task 6).
##
## Р”РІР° РїСЂР°РІРёР»Р°, СЂР°РґРё РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹С… СЌС‚РѕС‚ СѓР·РµР» СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚:
##   * РџРµСЂРµС…РѕРґС‹ РјРµР¶РґСѓ СЃС†РµРЅР°РјРё РёРґСѓС‚ РўРћР›Р¬РљРћ С‡РµСЂРµР· РїРѕРґРјРµРЅСЏРµРјС‹Р№ `scene_changer`.
##     РЎРїРµРєР° В§6.1 Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰Р°РµС‚ РїСЂСЏРјРѕР№ change_scene_to_file РІ РёРіСЂРѕРІС‹С… СЃС†РµРЅР°С…: СЃРјРµРЅР°
##     СЃС†РµРЅС‹ РёР· СЃРµСЂРµРґРёРЅС‹ РёРіСЂРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РєРѕРґР° РґРµР»Р°РµС‚ РїРµСЂРµС…РѕРґ РЅРµРІРёРґРёРјС‹Рј РґР»СЏ С‚РµСЃС‚РѕРІ Рё
##     РґР»СЏ С‚РѕРіРѕ, РєС‚Рѕ С‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚ РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°СЋС‰РёР№ РєРѕРґ. Р—РґРµСЃСЊ СЌС‚Рѕ РµРґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅР°СЏ РґРІРµСЂСЊ, Рё РІ
##     С‚РµСЃС‚Р°С… РѕРЅР° РїРѕРґРјРµРЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ Р·Р°РїРёСЃС‹РІР°СЋС‰РµР№ Р·Р°РіР»СѓС€РєРѕР№.
##   * РљР°Р¶РґРѕРµ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРµ (РѕС‡РєРё, РїРѕРєСѓРїРєР°, СЌРєРёРїРёСЂРѕРІРєР°) Р°РІС‚РѕСЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ С‡РµСЂРµР·
##     /root/SaveSystem, РµСЃР»Рё РѕРЅ РµСЃС‚СЊ. Null-safe: СЃС†РµРЅР° РёР»Рё С‚РµСЃС‚ Р±РµР·
##     Р°РІС‚РѕР·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ РЅРёС‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ Рё РЅРµ РїР°РґР°РµС‚.
##
## Р§РёСЃР»Р° РѕС‡РєРѕРІ вЂ” РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃ РІР»Р°РґРµР»СЊС†Р° (PROJECT.md В§7.5): СѓР±РёР№СЃС‚РІРѕ 10,
## РІРѕР»РЅР° 50, Р·Р°С‡РёСЃС‚РєР° 500. Р’РѕР»РЅС‹ Рё Р·Р°С‡РёСЃС‚РєР° РЅР°С‡РЅСѓС‚ РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»СЏС‚СЊСЃСЏ РІ Р¤Р°Р·Рµ 4;
## РєРѕРЅСЃС‚Р°РЅС‚С‹ Р·Р°РґР°РЅС‹ СѓР¶Рµ Р·РґРµСЃСЊ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ Р¤Р°Р·Рµ 4 РЅРµ РїСЂРёС€Р»РѕСЃСЊ РёС… РёР·РѕР±СЂРµС‚Р°С‚СЊ.

const POINTS_PER_KILL := 10
const POINTS_PER_WAVE := 50
const POINTS_PER_CLEAR := 500

## id Р»РѕРєР°С†РёРё -> СЃС†РµРЅР°. РџСЂРёРІР°С‚РЅС‹Р№: РЅР°СЂСѓР¶Сѓ С‚РѕСЂС‡РёС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ enter_location(id).
## "range" вЂ” РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ Р±РѕР№ С‡РµСЂРµР· РґРІРµСЂСЊ Р»РѕР±Р±Рё; РЅР°СЃС‚РѕСЏС‰Р°СЏ Р»РѕРєР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕСЏРІРёС‚СЃСЏ РІ
## Р¤Р°Р·Рµ 4, Рё С‚РѕРіРґР° РєР°СЂС‚Р° РІС‹СЂР°СЃС‚РµС‚, Р° РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°СЋС‰РёР№ РєРѕРґ РЅРµ РёР·РјРµРЅРёС‚СЃСЏ.
const _LOCATION_SCENES := {
	"range": "res://range.tscn",
}

var points: int = 0
var owned_weapons: PackedStringArray = ["pistol"]
var equipped_weapon_id: String = "pistol"
var unlocked_locations: PackedStringArray = ["range"]

## РџРѕРґРјРµРЅСЏРµРјС‹Р№ РІ С‚РµСЃС‚Р°С…. Р—РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЃСЏ РІ `_ready()`, Р° РќР• РІ
## `_init()`: РїСЂРё СЂСѓС‡РЅРѕРј `GameState.new()` + `add_child()` (СЃС†РµРЅР° РёР»Рё С‚РµСЃС‚ Р±РµР·
## Р°РІС‚РѕР·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё) `get_tree()` РІ `_init()` РµС‰С‘ null, Рё Callable СѓРєР°Р·С‹РІР°Р» Р±С‹ РІ
## РїСѓСЃС‚РѕС‚Сѓ. РРЅСЉРµРєС†РёСЏ РёР· С‚РµСЃС‚Р° СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЃСЏ Р”Рћ add_child, РїРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ `_ready()`
## Р·Р°РїРѕР»РЅСЏРµС‚ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РµРіРѕ РЅРёРєС‚Рѕ РЅРµ Р·Р°РґР°Р» (СЃРј. С‚Р°Рј Р¶Рµ).
var scene_changer: Callable


func _ready() -> void:
	# Р—Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚СЊ РґРІРёР¶РєРѕРІС‹Р№ РїРµСЂРµС…РѕРґ, С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РµРіРѕ РЅРёРєС‚Рѕ РЅРµ РїРѕРґРјРµРЅРёР». Р”РµС„РѕР»С‚РЅС‹Р№
	# Callable РЅРµРІР°Р»РёРґРµРЅ, РїРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ is_valid() РѕС‚Р»РёС‡Р°РµС‚ В«РЅРµ Р·Р°РґР°РЅВ» РѕС‚ Р·Р°РіР»СѓС€РєРё,
	# РїСЂРёСЃРІРѕРµРЅРЅРѕР№ РґРѕ РІС…РѕРґР° РІ РґРµСЂРµРІРѕ.
	if not scene_changer.is_valid():
		scene_changer = Callable(get_tree(), "change_scene_to_file")
	# Р’РѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїСЂРё РЅР°Р»РёС‡РёРё SaveSystem: РѕРЅ РїРѕСЏРІР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РєР°Рє Р°РІС‚РѕР·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР°
	# РІ Task 6, Р° РґРѕ С‚РµС… РїРѕСЂ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚СѓРµС‚ СЃ РґРµС„РѕР»С‚РѕРІ.
	var ss := get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSystem")
	if ss != null:
		_restore(ss.load_state())


## Р¦РµРЅР° РѕСЂСѓР¶РёСЏ РёР· РµРіРѕ СЂРµСЃСѓСЂСЃР°. РќРµС‚ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° РёР»Рё Р±РёС‚С‹Р№ СЂРµСЃСѓСЂСЃ вЂ” 0: РІРёС‚СЂРёРЅР° РґРѕР»Р¶РЅР°
## РїРѕРєР°Р·Р°С‚СЊ С†РµРЅСѓ, РЅРµ РїР°РґР°СЏ РЅР° РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РµРј РѕСЂСѓР¶РёРё. РћС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РІРѕРїСЂРѕСЃ вЂ” РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ Р»Рё
## С‚Р°РєРѕРµ РѕСЂСѓР¶РёРµ РєСѓРїРёС‚СЊ; РЅР° РЅРµРіРѕ РѕС‚РІРµС‡Р°РµС‚ buy_weapon, Р° РЅРµ С†РµРЅР°.
func weapon_price(id: String) -> int:
	var data := _load_weapon(id)
	return data.price if data != null else 0


## РќР°С‡РёСЃР»РµРЅРёРµ РѕС‡РєРѕРІ. РћС‚СЂРёС†Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ: СЌС‚Рѕ РЅРµ В«С€С‚СЂР°С„В», Р°
## Р·Р°С‰РёС‚Р° РѕС‚ РѕРїРµС‡Р°С‚РєРё РІ РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°СЋС‰РµРј РєРѕРґРµ, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂР°СЏ С‚РёС…Рѕ СѓРјРµРЅСЊС€РёР»Р° Р±С‹ СЃС‡С‘С‚.
func award_points(amount: int) -> void:
	if amount < 0:
		return
	points += amount
	_autosave()


func can_afford(cost: int) -> bool:
	return points >= cost


## РџРѕРєСѓРїРєР°. РЈР¶Рµ РєСѓРїР»РµРЅРЅРѕРµ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЌРєРёРїРёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ, Р±РµР· РІС‚РѕСЂРѕРіРѕ СЃРїРёСЃР°РЅРёСЏ. РџСЂРё РЅРµС…РІР°С‚РєРµ
## РѕС‡РєРѕРІ вЂ” РѕС‚РєР°Р· Р±РµР· РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёР№. РќРµСЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РµРµ РѕСЂСѓР¶РёРµ РєСѓРїРёС‚СЊ РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ: РёРЅР°С‡Рµ
## РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІРёРµ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° (weapon_price == 0) РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°Р»Рѕ Р±С‹ Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅСѓСЋ РїРѕРєСѓРїРєСѓ С‚РѕРіРѕ,
## С‡РµРіРѕ РЅРµС‚.
func buy_weapon(id: String) -> bool:
	if id in owned_weapons:
		return equip_weapon(id)
	var data := _load_weapon(id)
	if data == null:
		return false
	if not can_afford(data.price):
		return false
	points -= data.price
	owned_weapons.append(id)
	equipped_weapon_id = id
	_autosave()
	return true


## Р­РєРёРїРёСЂРѕРІРєР° С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РёР· РєСѓРїР»РµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ.
func equip_weapon(id: String) -> bool:
	if not (id in owned_weapons):
		return false
	equipped_weapon_id = id
	_autosave()
	return true


## Р’С…РѕРґ РІ Р»РѕРєР°С†РёСЋ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РёР· РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚С‹С… Рё С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РЅР° РёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅСѓСЋ СЃС†РµРЅСѓ; РёРЅР°С‡Рµ РѕС‚РєР°Р·
## Р‘Р•Р— СЃРјРµРЅС‹ СЃС†РµРЅС‹.
func enter_location(id: String) -> bool:
	if not (id in unlocked_locations):
		return false
	if not _LOCATION_SCENES.has(id):
		return false
	scene_changer.call(_LOCATION_SCENES[id])
	return true


## Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‚ РІ Р»РѕР±Р±Рё. reason РїРѕРєР° РЅРµ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ вЂ” РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂ РїРѕРґ Р±СѓРґСѓС‰РёРµ СЌРєСЂР°РЅС‹
## (В«СЃРјРµСЂС‚СЊВ», В«РІС‹С…РѕРґ РёР· Р±РѕСЏВ»), С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ СЃРёРіРЅР°С‚СѓСЂСѓ РЅРµ РїСЂРёС€Р»РѕСЃСЊ РјРµРЅСЏС‚СЊ Р·Р°РґРЅРёРј С‡РёСЃР»РѕРј.
@warning_ignore("unused_parameter")
func return_to_lobby(reason: String) -> void:
	scene_changer.call("res://lobby.tscn")


## РЎР»РѕРІР°СЂСЊ РґР»СЏ SaveSystem: СЂРѕРІРЅРѕ С‚Рѕ, С‡С‚Рѕ РїРµСЂРµР¶РёРІР°РµС‚ РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїСѓСЃРє. Packed-РјР°СЃСЃРёРІС‹
## РєРѕРЅРІРµСЂС‚РёСЂСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ РІ РѕР±С‹С‡РЅС‹Рµ Array вЂ” JSON-СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјС‹Р№ РІРёРґ, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ load_state()
## РІРµСЂРЅС‘С‚ РєР°Рє Array.
func _persist() -> Dictionary:
	return {
		"points": points,
		"owned_weapons": Array(owned_weapons),
		"equipped_weapon_id": equipped_weapon_id,
		"unlocked_locations": Array(unlocked_locations),
	}


## РџСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїСЂРёСЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РёРµ РєР»СЋС‡Рё Рё С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРѕРґС…РѕРґСЏС‰РёС… С‚РёРїРѕРІ: Р±РёС‚С‹Р№ РёР»Рё
## С‡Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ СЃРµР№РІ (РёР»Рё СЃРµР№РІ РёР· РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№ РІРµСЂСЃРёРё РёРіСЂС‹) РЅРµ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СЂРѕРЅСЏС‚СЊ
## РІРѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ Рё РЅРµ РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ Р·Р°С‚РёСЂР°С‚СЊ РґРµС„РѕР»С‚ РјСѓСЃРѕСЂРѕРј.
func _restore(saved: Dictionary) -> void:
	if saved.has("points") and (saved["points"] is int or saved["points"] is float):
		points = int(saved["points"])
	if saved.has("owned_weapons") and saved["owned_weapons"] is Array:
		owned_weapons = _to_string_array(saved["owned_weapons"])
	if saved.has("equipped_weapon_id") and saved["equipped_weapon_id"] is String:
		equipped_weapon_id = saved["equipped_weapon_id"]
	if saved.has("unlocked_locations") and saved["unlocked_locations"] is Array:
		unlocked_locations = _to_string_array(saved["unlocked_locations"])


## РђРІС‚РѕСЃРµР№РІ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ РјСѓС‚Р°С‚РѕСЂР°. Null-safe: Р±РµР· Р°РІС‚РѕР·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё SaveSystem
## СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏС‚СЊ РЅРµРєСѓРґР°, Рё СЌС‚Рѕ РЅРµ РѕС€РёР±РєР°.
func _autosave() -> void:
	var ss := get_node_or_null("/root/SaveSystem")
	if ss != null and ss.has_method("save"):
		ss.save(_persist())


## Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° СЂРµСЃСѓСЂСЃР° РѕСЂСѓР¶РёСЏ РїРѕ id. РџСѓСЃС‚РѕР№ id РёР»Рё РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓСЋС‰РёР№ С„Р°Р№Р» вЂ” null.
func _load_weapon(id: String) -> WeaponData:
	if id.is_empty():
		return null
	return load("res://data/weapons/%s.tres" % id) as WeaponData


## Array РёР· JSON -> PackedStringArray. РќРµ-СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё РѕС‚Р±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ, С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ Р±РёС‚С‹Р№ СЃРµР№РІ
## РЅРµ РјРѕРі РїРѕР»РѕР¶РёС‚СЊ РІ РјРµС€РѕРє РѕСЂСѓР¶РёСЏ РјСѓСЃРѕСЂ С‡СѓР¶РѕРіРѕ С‚РёРїР°.
func _to_string_array(value: Array) -> PackedStringArray:
	var out := PackedStringArray()
	for item in value:
		if item is String:
			out.append(item)
	return out

## Р’РёС‚СЂРёРЅР° Р»РѕР±Р±Рё: С‚РѕС‡РєР° РїРѕРєСѓРїРєРё РѕСЂСѓР¶РёСЏ. РЎС‚РѕРёС‚ РІ РєРѕРјРЅР°С‚Рµ, РёРіСЂРѕРє РїРѕРґС…РѕРґРёС‚ РІРїР»РѕС‚РЅСѓСЋ
## Рё Р¶РјС‘С‚ E; interact() РїСЂРѕРІРѕРґРёС‚ РїРѕРєСѓРїРєСѓ С‡РµСЂРµР· GameState Рё РіРѕРІРѕСЂРёС‚, СѓРґР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ Р»Рё
## РѕРЅР°. РЎР»РѕР№ 6 (interactable), РјР°СЃРєР° вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РёРіСЂРѕРє: С‚РѕС‡РєР° РґРѕР»Р¶РЅР° РІРёРґРµС‚СЊ РёРіСЂРѕРєР°
## СЃРІРѕРёРј С‚СЂРёРіРіРµСЂРѕРј, РЅРѕ РЅРµ СЃС‚Р°Р»РєРёРІР°С‚СЊСЃСЏ РЅРё СЃ С‡РµРј РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рј.
##
## interact(player) РґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ player С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЂР°РґРё РµРґРёРЅРѕРіРѕ РїСЂРѕС‚РѕРєРѕР»Р° РІР·Р°РёРјРѕРґРµР№СЃС‚РІРёСЏ
## (Сѓ DoorPoint СЃРёРіРЅР°С‚СѓСЂР° С‚Р° Р¶Рµ) вЂ” РїРѕРєСѓРїР°РµС‚ СЃР°РјР° РїРѕРєСѓРїРєР°, Р° РЅРµ РёРіСЂРѕРє.

## РџРѕРєСѓРїРєР° СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏР»Р°СЃСЊ (РёР»Рё Р±С‹Р»Р° РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅРµРЅР°). РЎРёРіРЅР°Р» РѕР± РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёРё: HUD Рё С‚РµСЃС‚С‹
## СѓР·РЅР°СЋС‚ Рѕ СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚Рµ, РЅРµ РѕРїСЂР°С€РёРІР°СЏ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ.
signal interacted(ok: bool)

## РљР°РєРѕРµ РѕСЂСѓР¶РёРµ РїСЂРѕРґР°С‘С‚ СЌС‚Р° РІРёС‚СЂРёРЅР°. РРјСЏ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° РІ data/weapons Р±РµР· СЂР°СЃС€РёСЂРµРЅРёСЏ.
@export var weapon_id: String = "auto"


func _ready() -> void:
	collision_layer = 1 << 5
	collision_mask = 1 << 1
	monitoring = true
	add_to_group("interactable")


@warning_ignore("unused_parameter")
func interact(player: Node) -> bool:
	var gs := get_node_or_null("/root/GameState") as GameState
	if gs == null:
		return false
	var ok := gs.buy_weapon(weapon_id)
	interacted.emit(ok)
	return ok

## (interactable), С‚СЂРёРіРіРµСЂ РІРёРґРёС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РёРіСЂРѕРєР°. interact() Р·РѕРІС‘С‚
## GameState.enter_location(location_id), РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Р№ СЃР°Рј СЂРµС€Р°РµС‚, РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚Р° Р»Рё
## Р»РѕРєР°С†РёСЏ, Рё СѓС…РѕРґРёС‚ РІ СЃРјРµРЅСѓ СЃС†РµРЅС‹ С‡РµСЂРµР· РїРѕРґРјРµРЅСЏРµРјС‹Р№ scene_changer.

--- FILE: tests/save.gd ---
extends SceneTree

## SaveSystem harness for VibeFPS.
## Exercises SaveSystem against an isolated user:// path (save_test.json) so the
## production save file (user://save.json) is never touched: no save on a clean
## start, a save/load round-trip, clear_save(), a load from a missing file, a
## load from a corrupted file, and a write that must fail (path is a directory).
## Run: godot --headless --path . --script res://tests/save.gd
##      .\tools\run_checks.ps1 save

## Isolated path every test interaction uses. The production path
## (user://save.json) is intentionally never touched by this harness.
const _TMP_PATH := "user://save_test.json"
## A directory in user:// whose path save() must refuse to write to.
const _TMP_DIR := "user://save_test_dir"

var _failures: PackedStringArray = []
var _warnings: PackedStringArray = []
var _checks := 0
var _save: SaveSystem


func _check(ok: bool, label: String, detail: String = "") -> void:
	_checks += 1
	if ok:
		print("  [PASS] %s %s" % [label, detail])
	else:
		_failures.append("%s %s" % [label, detail])
		print("  [FAIL] %s %s" % [label, detail])


func _warn(label: String) -> void:
	_warnings.append(label)
	print("  [WARN] %s" % label)


## Clean sheet before and after the run: neither the test file nor the test
## directory may survive into a later run. Removing a path that does not exist
## is a non-event (remove_absolute just reports an error, which we ignore).
func _cleanup() -> void:
	DirAccess.remove_absolute(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(_TMP_PATH))
	DirAccess.remove_absolute(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(_TMP_DIR))


## Overwrites the test path with arbitrary text (malformed JSON or valid JSON
## of a non-object shape) to exercise load-time resilience.
func _write_file(text: String) -> void:
	var file := FileAccess.open(_TMP_PATH, FileAccess.WRITE)
	if file == null:
		_warn("cannot open %s to write test content" % _TMP_PATH)
		return
	file.store_string(text)
	file.close()


func _initialize() -> void:
	print("=== save system test ===")
	_cleanup()

	_save = SaveSystem.new()
	_save.save_path = _TMP_PATH
	root.add_child(_save)
	await physics_frame

	var payload := { "points": 42, "equipped": "auto" }

	_check(not _save.has_save(), "no save on a clean start")

	var saved := _save.save(payload)
	_check(saved, "save() accepts a payload and reports success")
	await physics_frame
	_check(_save.has_save(), "has_save() is true after save()")

	var loaded := _save.load_state()
	# Whole-dictionary == is intentionally not used: Godot's JSON.parse_string
	# reads every number back as a float ("points" comes back 42.0), and
	# Dictionary equality is type-strict, so `loaded == { "points": 42, ... }`
	# would be false even though the value round-trips correctly. Value-level
	# comparison is the behavior being tested here.
	_check(loaded.size() == payload.size(),
		"load_state() returns the same number of keys as saved", "(got %s)" % loaded)
	# .get() with a fallback, not [] indexing: a regression that drops a key must
	# surface as a plain [FAIL] here, not as a runtime error that aborts the
	# harness (_initialize never reaches quit(), and Godot idles until the
	# runner's 120 s timeout kills the check).
	_check(loaded.get("points") == payload["points"],
		"load_state() round-trips the points value",
		"(got %s, expected %s)" % [loaded.get("points"), payload["points"]])
	_check(loaded.get("equipped") == payload["equipped"],
		"load_state() round-trips the equipped value",
		"(got %s)" % loaded.get("equipped"))

	_save.clear_save()
	await physics_frame
	_check(not _save.has_save(), "has_save() is false after clear_save()")
	_check(_save.load_state() == {},
		"load_state() on a missing file returns an empty dict")

	_write_file("{ not valid json")
	await physics_frame
	_check(_save.load_state() == {},
		"load_state() on a corrupted file returns an empty dict without crashing")

	# "{ not valid json" fails to parse at all (JSON.parse_string -> null, which
	# a `-> Dictionary` return silently coerces to {}). The `is Dictionary` guard
	# in load_state() is only load-bearing when the JSON parses but has a
	# non-object shape, so that case is exercised too.
	_write_file("[1, 2, 3]")
	await physics_frame
	_check(_save.load_state() == {},
		"load_state() on valid JSON that is not an object returns an empty dict")

	await _test_unwritable_path()

	_save.clear_save()
	_cleanup()

	print("=== RESULT: %d checks, %d failed, %d warnings ===" % [
		_checks, _failures.size(), _warnings.size()])
	for f in _failures:
		print("  FAILURE: %s" % f)
	quit(1 if _failures.size() > 0 else 0)


## save() must not pretend a write succeeded when the path points at a
## directory that can never be written as a file.
func _test_unwritable_path() -> void:
	print("-- save() against an unwritable path (a directory) must fail cleanly")
	var status := DirAccess.make_dir_recursive_absolute(
		ProjectSettings.globalize_path(_TMP_DIR))
	if status != OK:
		_warn("cannot create %s (error %d)" % [_TMP_DIR, status])
		return
	_save.save_path = _TMP_DIR
	await physics_frame
	_check(not _save.save({ "points": 1 }),
		"save() returns false when the path is a directory")
	_save.save_path = _TMP_PATH

--- FILE: tests/auto_weapon.gd ---
extends SceneTree

## Auto-weapon harness for VibeFPS: the second weapon (an automatic pistol) is
## the same pistol.gd behaviour driven by a different WeaponData, so this check
## pins the parts that differ -- the data itself, the scene wiring, the shot
## through the real projectile.tscn -- and the projectile visual that has to
## follow the weapon data (radius 0.06, yellow trail) instead of the pistol's
## defaults.
##
## The pattern is borrowed from tests/weapon.gd: a _PistolProbe answers the
## input gate with an explicit mouse mode (headless cannot capture the cursor),
## the shipped auto.tscn geometry is grafted onto the probe, and waits are
## bounded in wall-clock seconds because the weapon's rate limit is the thing
## under test and lives in seconds, not frames.
##
## Run: godot --headless --path . --script res://tests/auto_weapon.gd
##      .\tools\run_checks.ps1 auto_weapon

## Stand-in for pistol.gd, used only by this harness.
class _PistolProbe extends "res://scripts/pistol.gd":
	## Mouse mode the gate is evaluated against instead of Input.mouse_mode.
	var gate_mouse_mode: int = Input.MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED

	func _mouse_mode_for_play_input() -> int:
		return gate_mouse_mode


## Records what the weapon announces. fired counts shots -- one signal, one
## round, which is what "the auto really shoots" reduces to here; the full
## magazine bookkeeping is already pinned for pistol.gd by tests/weapon.gd.
class _Log extends RefCounted:
	var fired: int = 0

	func _on_fired() -> void:
		fired += 1


const _AUTO_DATA_PATH := "res://data/weapons/auto.tres"
const _AUTO_SCENE_PATH := "res://auto.tscn"
## The projectile scene auto.tscn points at, loaded rather than rebuilt: the
## trail under data-driven test is the trail the shipped scene really carries.
const _PROJECTILE_SCENE_PATH := "res://projectile.tscn"

## The values the design fixes for the auto. Restated here so a balance edit to
## auto.tres cannot silently turn the harness into a check of numbers it does
## not claim to verify.
const _EXPECTED_MAGAZINE := 36
const _EXPECTED_INTERVAL := 0.2
const _EXPECTED_DAMAGE := 15.0
const _EXPECTED_SPEED := 70.0
const _EXPECTED_RADIUS := 0.06
const _EXPECTED_PRICE := 300
const _EXPECTED_COLOR := Color(0.95, 0.75, 0.15)

## Ceiling for every timed wait; a broken weapon leaves it far behind, a hung
## one cannot turn the check into a runner timeout.
const _WAIT_CAP_SECONDS := 15.0
## How far a measured timer may overshoot the nominal interval.
const _TIMER_TOLERANCE := 0.35
## Float slack on the short side of a timer.
const _TIMER_EPSILON := 0.05

var _failures: PackedStringArray = []
var _checks := 0
var _data: WeaponData
var _projectile_scene: PackedScene


func _check(ok: bool, label: String, detail: String = "") -> void:
	_checks += 1
	if ok:
		print("  [PASS] %s %s" % [label, detail])
	else:
		_failures.append("%s %s" % [label, detail])
		print("  [FAIL] %s %s" % [label, detail])


func _finish() -> void:
	print("=== RESULT: %d checks, %d failed ===" % [_checks, _failures.size()])
	for f in _failures:
		print("  FAILURE: %s" % f)


func _bail(reason: String) -> void:
	print("FATAL: %s" % reason)
	_finish()
	quit(1)


func _close(a: float, b: float) -> bool:
	return is_equal_approx(a, b)


func _close_color(a: Color, b: Color) -> bool:
	return is_equal_approx(a.r, b.r) and is_equal_approx(a.g, b.g) \
		and is_equal_approx(a.b, b.b)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# waiting
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

func _await_until(predicate: Callable, max_seconds := _WAIT_CAP_SECONDS) -> bool:
	var deadline := _now_ms() + int(max_seconds * 1000.0)
	var reached := false
	for i in 1000000:
		await process_frame
		if predicate.call():
			reached = true
			break
		if _now_ms() >= deadline:
			break
	return reached


func _now_ms() -> int:
	return Time.get_ticks_msec()


## Releases the fire action and lets real frames go by, so the arming latch has
## seen the button up before the next press is read as input.
func _settle_fire_released() -> void:
	Input.action_release("fire")
	await process_frame
	await process_frame


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# world builders
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

func _new_world() -> Node3D:
	var world := Node3D.new()
	world.name = "World"
	root.add_child(world)
	return world


## A current camera in the pose a player's head hangs at, standing in for
## player.tscn. auto.tscn leaves fire_rate_source empty on purpose, so this is
## the node the weapon actually aims from in the game.
func _new_eye(world: Node3D) -> Camera3D:
	var eye := Camera3D.new()
	eye.name = "Eye"
	eye.current = true
	eye.position = Vector3(0.0, 1.7, 0.0)
	world.add_child(eye)
	return eye


## The shipped auto geometry grafted onto a probe. Children are MOVED over, so
## the Muzzle marker under test is the one auto.tscn really ships; the probe is
## what answers the gate (see the class), and data plus projectile scene are
## set before the node enters the tree because _ready() fills the magazine from
## them.
func _new_pistol_from_scene(world: Node3D, scene: PackedScene, data: WeaponData) -> _PistolProbe:
	var geometry := scene.instantiate()
	var pistol := _PistolProbe.new()
	pistol.name = "Auto"
	pistol.data = data
	pistol.projectile_scene = _projectile_scene
	for child in geometry.get_children():
		geometry.remove_child(child)
		child.owner = null
		pistol.add_child(child)
	geometry.free()
	world.add_child(pistol)
	for i in 8:
		await process_frame
		if pistol.is_node_ready():
			return pistol
	print("FATAL: the auto never became ready")
	return pistol


func _watch(pistol: _PistolProbe) -> _Log:
	var log := _Log.new()
	pistol.fired.connect(log._on_fired)
	return log


## The projectiles under `parent`. The weapon parents every shot to its aim
## source (here: the camera), so this is how live bullets are found.
func _live_projectiles(parent: Node3D) -> Array[Projectile]:
	var out: Array[Projectile] = []
	for child in parent.get_children():
		if child is Projectile:
			out.append(child as Projectile)
	return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# entry point
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

func _initialize() -> void:
	print("=== VibeFPS auto weapon test ===")
	_data = load(_AUTO_DATA_PATH) as WeaponData
	if _data == null:
		_bail("cannot load %s as a WeaponData" % _AUTO_DATA_PATH)
		return
	_projectile_scene = load(_PROJECTILE_SCENE_PATH) as PackedScene
	if _projectile_scene == null:
		_bail("cannot load %s" % _PROJECTILE_SCENE_PATH)
		return

	_test_data_contract()
	_test_scene_contract()
	await _test_scene_shot()
	await _test_fire_interval()

	_finish()
	quit(1 if _failures.size() > 0 else 0)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# checks
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

## The data the auto is defined by. A balance edit to auto.tres has to turn a
## check red, never a scene or a hardcode.
func _test_data_contract() -> void:
	print("-- data: auto.tres is the auto the design fixes")
	_check(_data.is_valid(), "auto.tres reports itself valid")
	_check(_data.display_name == "Auto", "display_name is \"Auto\"",
		"(got \"%s\")" % _data.display_name)
	_check(_close(_data.seconds_between_shots(), _EXPECTED_INTERVAL),
		"seconds_between_shots() is %.1f s at 300 rpm" % _EXPECTED_INTERVAL,
		"(got %.6f)" % _data.seconds_between_shots())
	_check(_data.magazine_size == _EXPECTED_MAGAZINE,
		"magazine_size is %d" % _EXPECTED_MAGAZINE,
		"(got %d)" % _data.magazine_size)
	_check(_close(_data.damage, _EXPECTED_DAMAGE),
		"damage is %.1f" % _EXPECTED_DAMAGE, "(got %.4f)" % _data.damage)
	_check(_data.price == _EXPECTED_PRICE,
		"price is %d" % _EXPECTED_PRICE, "(got %d)" % _data.price)
	_check(_close(_data.projectile_speed, _EXPECTED_SPEED),
		"projectile_speed is %.1f m/s" % _EXPECTED_SPEED,
		"(got %.4f)" % _data.projectile_speed)
	_check(_close(_data.projectile_radius, _EXPECTED_RADIUS),
		"projectile_radius is %.2f" % _EXPECTED_RADIUS,
		"(got %.4f)" % _data.projectile_radius)
	_check(_close_color(_data.projectile_color, _EXPECTED_COLOR),
		"projectile_color is the auto's yellow", "(got %s)" % _data.projectile_color)


## The scene wiring: auto.tscn is the auto with a barrel, carrying the auto
## data and the projectile scene, and no fire_rate_source (the weapon falls
## back to the player's camera exactly like the pistol).
func _test_scene_contract() -> void:
	print("-- scene: auto.tscn instantiates and is wired to auto.tres")
	var packed: PackedScene = load(_AUTO_SCENE_PATH)
	_check(packed != null, "auto.tscn is loadable", "(%s)" % _AUTO_SCENE_PATH)
	if packed == null:
		return
	var direct := packed.instantiate()
	_check(direct != null, "auto.tscn instantiates")
	if direct == null:
		return
	_check(direct.get("data") == _data,
		"auto.tscn carries data = auto.tres")
	_check(direct.get("projectile_scene") == _projectile_scene,
		"auto.tscn drives res://projectile.tscn")
	_check(direct.get("fire_rate_source") == null,
		"auto.tscn leaves fire_rate_source empty: it aims from the camera")
	var barrel := direct.get_node_or_null("Muzzle") as Node3D
	_check(barrel != null, "auto.tscn carries a Muzzle marker")
	_check(barrel != null and barrel.position.z < -0.1,
		"and it sits in front of the weapon, not inside the grip",
		"(got %s)" % ("no Muzzle" if barrel == null else str(barrel.position)))
	direct.free()


## One real shot through the input path with the scene grafted onto the probe:
## the round leaves the barrel, flies at the auto's speed, and the projectile
## instance carries the data -- speed, damage, and the visual radius/color the
## trail is built from. The trail of the shipped projectile.tscn is checked
## too, because that is the "projectile look from data" contract end to end.
func _test_scene_shot() -> void:
	print("-- shot: one round, aimed by a camera, projectile look from auto.tres")
	var world := _new_world()
	var eye := _new_eye(world)
	var packed: PackedScene = load(_AUTO_SCENE_PATH)
	if packed == null:
		world.free()
		return
	var pistol := await _new_pistol_from_scene(world, packed, _data)
	var log := _watch(pistol)

	var barrel := pistol.get_node_or_null("Muzzle") as Node3D
	_check(barrel != null, "precondition: the grafted scene carries its Muzzle marker")

	await _settle_fire_released()
	Input.action_press("fire")
	var fired := await _await_until(func() -> bool: return log.fired > 0, 5.0)
	Input.action_release("fire")
	_check(fired, "the held fire button produced a shot", "(got %d)" % log.fired)
	_check(log.fired == 1, "exactly one shot came out", "(got %d)" % log.fired)
	_check(pistol.current_mag() == _EXPECTED_MAGAZINE - 1,
		"the shot cost exactly one round", "(got %d)" % pistol.current_mag())

	var bullets := _live_projectiles(eye)
	_check(bullets.size() == 1, "one projectile was spawned under the camera",
		"(got %d)" % bullets.size())
	if bullets.size() == 1 and barrel != null:
		var bullet := bullets[0]
		# Sample late by design: the shot came through the input path, so the
		# projectile has already flown part of its line by the time the harness
		# sees it. What the start point is, is still decidable: a bullet that
		# left the eye would sit at eye height (~1.7 m), one that left the
		# barrel hangs at the barrel's height (~0 m) on the camera's line.
		_check(bullet.global_position.distance_to(eye.global_position) > 0.5,
			"the projectile is not hanging out of the eye",
			"(%.3f m from the eye)" % bullet.global_position.distance_to(eye.global_position))
		_check(absf(bullet.global_position.y - barrel.global_position.y) < 0.2,
			"it left from the barrel height, not the camera height",
			"(bullet y=%.3f, barrel y=%.3f, eye y=%.3f)" % [
				bullet.global_position.y, barrel.global_position.y, eye.global_position.y])
		_check(_close(bullet.speed, _EXPECTED_SPEED),
			"the projectile takes the auto's speed from the data",
			"(got %.1f m/s)" % bullet.speed)
		_check(_close(bullet.damage, _EXPECTED_DAMAGE),
			"the projectile takes the auto's damage from the data",
			"(got %.1f)" % bullet.damage)
		_check(_close(bullet.radius, _EXPECTED_RADIUS),
			"the projectile carries the auto's radius from the data",
			"(got %.4f)" % bullet.radius)
		_check(_close_color(bullet.color, _data.projectile_color),
			"the projectile carries the auto's color from the data",
			"(got %s, want %s)" % [str(bullet.color), str(_data.projectile_color)])

		var trail := bullet.get_node_or_null("Trail") as MeshInstance3D
		_check(trail != null, "the projectile carries a trail node")
		if trail != null:
			var sphere := trail.mesh as SphereMesh
			_check(sphere != null and _close(sphere.radius, bullet.radius),
				"the trail sphere has the data radius, so the visual follows auto.tres",
				"(got %s)" % ("no SphereMesh" if sphere == null else "%.4f" % sphere.radius))
			var mat := trail.material_override as StandardMaterial3D
			_check(mat != null and _close_color(mat.albedo_color, _data.projectile_color),
				"the trail material carries the data color",
				"(got %s)" % ("no material" if mat == null else str(mat.albedo_color)))

		var start: Vector3 = bullet.global_position
		await _await_until(func() -> bool: return bullet.global_position.distance_to(start) > 0.5, 5.0)
		var moved: Vector3 = bullet.global_position - start
		_check(moved.length() > 0.5,
			"the projectile is flying, so launch() was called on a node in the tree",
			"(moved %.3f m)" % moved.length())
		_check(moved.normalized().dot(-eye.global_transform.basis.z) > 0.99,
			"it leaves along the camera's forward axis",
			"(%.3f m along %.3f m forward)" % [
				moved.length(), moved.normalized().dot(-eye.global_transform.basis.z)])
	world.free()


## The rate limit lives in seconds and comes from the data: 300 rpm is one shot
## every 0.2 s, refused in the same instant and allowed once the interval has
## passed. The interval is measured on the scene-created weapon, so the whole
## chain (auto.tres -> pistol.gd -> actual timing) is under test, not just the
## resource's arithmetic.
func _test_fire_interval() -> void:
	print("-- rate limit: one shot per %.1f s, in seconds not frames" % _EXPECTED_INTERVAL)
	var world := _new_world()
	var packed: PackedScene = load(_AUTO_SCENE_PATH)
	if packed == null:
		world.free()
		return
	var pistol := await _new_pistol_from_scene(world, packed, _data)
	var log := _watch(pistol)

	_check(pistol.try_fire(), "the first shot is allowed")
	_check(not pistol.try_fire(), "a second shot in the same instant is refused")
	_check(log.fired == 1, "only one round left the magazine",
		"(got %d)" % log.fired)

	var start := _now_ms()
	var second := false
	for i in 6000:
		await process_frame
		if pistol.try_fire():
			second = true
			break
	var elapsed := float(_now_ms() - start) / 1000.0
	print("    second shot after %.3f s" % elapsed)
	_check(second, "the next shot is allowed once the interval has passed")
	_check(elapsed >= _EXPECTED_INTERVAL - _TIMER_EPSILON,
		"the second shot waited at least the full interval",
		"(waited %.3f s, want >= %.2f s)" % [elapsed, _EXPECTED_INTERVAL - _TIMER_EPSILON])
	_check(elapsed <= _EXPECTED_INTERVAL + _TIMER_TOLERANCE,
		"and did not wait much longer than the interval",
		"(waited %.3f s, want <= %.2f s)" % [elapsed, _EXPECTED_INTERVAL + _TIMER_TOLERANCE])
	_check(log.fired == 2, "exactly two rounds came out",
		"(got %d)" % log.fired)
	world.free()

--- FILE: tests/progression.gd ---
extends SceneTree

## Progression harness for VibeFPS (Phase 3, lobby track A): points, buying and
## equipping weapons, unlocked locations, scene transitions and the autosave
## round-trip through SaveSystem.
##
## The SaveSystem autoload does not exist yet (a later task wires it into
## project.godot), so the harness builds one by hand at user://progression_test.json
## -- the same isolation tests/save.gd uses, so the production user://save.json is
## never touched. Scene transitions go through an injected fake changer: headless
## has no window to swap scenes in, and the production scenes must not be loaded
## out from under the harness.
##
## The main instance (`_gs`) walks the whole progression. A second, clean-slate
## instance (`_roundtrip`) is used for the disk round-trip check: the brief fixes
## the expected value after award_points(50) at 50 points, which only holds from
## a fresh state. The main instance has already spent down to 10 points by then,
## so reusing it would make the same operation 60 and tie the round-trip check to
## the purchase arithmetic. Isolating it keeps both numbers exactly what the
## brief states.
##
## Run: godot --headless --path . --script res://tests/progression.gd
##      .\tools\run_checks.ps1 progression

## Isolated path every interaction uses. user://save.json is never touched.
const _TMP_PATH := "user://progression_test.json"

var _failures: PackedStringArray = []
var _checks := 0

var _save: SaveSystem
var _gs: GameState
## Clean-slate instance for the autosave round-trip (see the file header).
var _roundtrip: GameState
## Paths handed to the fake scene changer, in call order. No scene is actually
## swapped -- the fake only records what it was asked for.
var _changed_to: PackedStringArray = []


func _check(ok: bool, label: String, detail: String = "") -> void:
	_checks += 1
	if ok:
		print("  [PASS] %s %s" % [label, detail])
	else:
		_failures.append("%s %s" % [label, detail])
		print("  [FAIL] %s %s" % [label, detail])


## Fake scene changer: records the requested path and does nothing else. A real
## change_scene_to_file would tear down this very SceneTree under the harness.
func _fake_changer(path: String) -> void:
	_changed_to.append(path)


## PackedStringArray vs Array comparison by contents. Godot does not treat the
## two container types as equal, so a plain `owned == ["pistol"]` would be false
## even when the contents match.
func _packed_eq(packed: PackedStringArray, expected: Array) -> bool:
	if packed.size() != expected.size():
		return false
	for i in packed.size():
		if packed[i] != expected[i]:
			return false
	return true


func _cleanup() -> void:
	DirAccess.remove_absolute(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(_TMP_PATH))


## The engine registers GameState/SaveSystem as project autoloads
## (project.godot, Task 6) and installs nodes of those names under /root even in
## --script mode. Left in place they would shadow the harness's own nodes of the
## same name (Godot renames the later one, so /root/SaveSystem would resolve to
## the autoload writing user://save.json) and hand GameState the real
## change_scene_to_file. Drop them first, so the harness owns /root/GameState and
## /root/SaveSystem exactly as it did before the autoloads existed. Immediate
## free, not queue_free: the names must be free before the nodes below are added.
func _drop_autoloads() -> void:
	for autoload_name in ["GameState", "SaveSystem"]:
		var node := root.get_node_or_null(autoload_name)
		if node != null:
			root.remove_child(node)
			node.free()


func _initialize() -> void:
	print("=== VibeFPS progression test ===")
	_drop_autoloads()
	# Clean sheet: a leftover save from an interrupted run must not be restored
	# into the state under test.
	_cleanup()

	_save = SaveSystem.new()
	# The name is what makes /root/SaveSystem resolve for GameState's autosave.
	_save.name = "SaveSystem"
	_save.save_path = _TMP_PATH
	root.add_child(_save)

	_gs = GameState.new()
	# Injected before add_child, so GameState._ready() must not overwrite it.
	_gs.scene_changer = _fake_changer
	root.add_child(_gs)
	await physics_frame

	_test_initial_state()
	_test_award_points()
	_test_afford()
	_test_buy_weapon()
	_test_buy_owned_again()
	_test_buy_missing()
	_test_equip()
	_test_enter_location()
	_test_return_to_lobby()
	await _test_autosave_roundtrip()

	_save.clear_save()
	_cleanup()

	print("=== RESULT: %d checks, %d failed ===" % [_checks, _failures.size()])
	for f in _failures:
		print("  FAILURE: %s" % f)
	quit(1 if _failures.size() > 0 else 0)


func _test_initial_state() -> void:
	print("-- start: a fresh game starts with the pistol and the range")
	_check(_gs.points == 0, "points start at 0", "(got %d)" % _gs.points)
	_check(_packed_eq(_gs.owned_weapons, ["pistol"]),
		"only the pistol is owned", "(got %s)" % _gs.owned_weapons)
	_check(_gs.equipped_weapon_id == "pistol", "the pistol is equipped",
		"(got \"%s\")" % _gs.equipped_weapon_id)
	_check(_packed_eq(_gs.unlocked_locations, ["range"]),
		"only the range is unlocked", "(got %s)" % _gs.unlocked_locations)


func _test_award_points() -> void:
	print("-- award_points: adds, ignores negatives")
	_gs.award_points(10)
	_check(_gs.points == 10, "award_points(10) adds 10", "(got %d)" % _gs.points)
	_gs.award_points(-5)
	_check(_gs.points == 10, "award_points(-5) is ignored, not deducted",
		"(got %d)" % _gs.points)


func _test_afford() -> void:
	print("-- can_afford: the auto costs 300")
	_check(_gs.weapon_price("auto") == 300,
		"weapon_price(\"auto\") reads 300 from auto.tres",
		"(got %d)" % _gs.weapon_price("auto"))
	_check(not _gs.can_afford(300), "300 points cannot afford the 300 auto")
	_gs.award_points(300)
	_check(_gs.can_afford(300), "after award_points(300) the auto is affordable")


func _test_buy_weapon() -> void:
	print("-- buy_weapon: deducts the price, owns and equips")
	var bought := _gs.buy_weapon("auto")
	_check(bought, "buy_weapon(\"auto\") succeeds when affordable")
	_check(_gs.points == 10, "the 300 price was deducted (310 -> 10)",
		"(got %d)" % _gs.points)
	_check("auto" in _gs.owned_weapons, "\"auto\" is now owned",
		"(got %s)" % _gs.owned_weapons)
	_check(_gs.equipped_weapon_id == "auto", "and it is equipped",
		"(got \"%s\")" % _gs.equipped_weapon_id)


func _test_buy_owned_again() -> void:
	print("-- buy_weapon on an owned weapon: equips only, no second charge")
	var again := _gs.buy_weapon("auto")
	_check(again, "buying an already-owned weapon returns true")
	_check(_gs.points == 10, "and does not charge twice",
		"(got %d)" % _gs.points)
	_check(_gs.owned_weapons.size() == 2,
		"the weapon is not owned twice", "(got %s)" % _gs.owned_weapons)


## A weapon with no .tres reports price 0, but that must not make it buyable as
## a free item: "no resource" is a refusal, fixed here before the implementation
## is read.
func _test_buy_missing() -> void:
	print("-- buy_weapon(\"nope\"): a missing resource is not for sale")
	_check(_gs.weapon_price("nope") == 0,
		"weapon_price(\"nope\") falls back to 0",
		"(got %d)" % _gs.weapon_price("nope"))
	var bought := _gs.buy_weapon("nope")
	_check(not bought, "buy_weapon refuses a weapon with no .tres")
	_check(not ("nope" in _gs.owned_weapons), "and does not add it to the bag",
		"(got %s)" % _gs.owned_weapons)


func _test_equip() -> void:
	print("-- equip_weapon: only owned weapons")
	_check(_gs.equip_weapon("pistol"), "equipping the owned pistol returns true")
	_check(_gs.equipped_weapon_id == "pistol", "the pistol is equipped again",
		"(got \"%s\")" % _gs.equipped_weapon_id)
	_check(_gs.equip_weapon("auto"), "equipping the owned auto returns true")
	_check(_gs.equipped_weapon_id == "auto", "the auto is equipped",
		"(got \"%s\")" % _gs.equipped_weapon_id)
	_check(not _gs.equip_weapon("nope"), "equipping an unowned weapon is refused")
	_check(_gs.equipped_weapon_id == "auto",
		"and does not change the equipped weapon",
		"(got \"%s\")" % _gs.equipped_weapon_id)


func _test_enter_location() -> void:
	print("-- enter_location: unlocked ids only, through the injected changer")
	var entered := _gs.enter_location("range")
	_check(entered, "entering the unlocked range succeeds")
	_check(_packed_eq(_changed_to, ["res://range.tscn"]),
		"the changer was asked for res://range.tscn", "(got %s)" % _changed_to)
	var before := _changed_to.size()
	_check(not _gs.enter_location("secret"), "a locked location is refused")
	_check(_changed_to.size() == before,
		"and no scene change was requested", "(got %s)" % _changed_to)


func _test_return_to_lobby() -> void:
	print("-- return_to_lobby: back to the lobby through the changer")
	_gs.return_to_lobby("death")
	_check(_packed_eq(_changed_to, ["res://range.tscn", "res://lobby.tscn"]),
		"the changer was then asked for res://lobby.tscn",
		"(got %s)" % _changed_to)


func _test_autosave_roundtrip() -> void:
	print("-- autosave: mutations land on disk, and a fresh state reads back")
	# The buy and equip above must already be on disk (autosave after every
	# mutator): points 10, auto equipped.
	var disk := _save.load_state()
	_check(int(disk.get("points", -1)) == 10,
		"autosave after buy/equip persisted points == 10",
		"(got %s)" % disk.get("points"))
	_check(disk.get("equipped_weapon_id") == "auto",
		"autosave persisted the equipped weapon",
		"(got %s)" % disk.get("equipped_weapon_id"))

	# Clean-slate round-trip: award_points(50) on a fresh state, then read the
	# value back from disk. The brief fixes this number at 50.
	_save.clear_save()
	_roundtrip = GameState.new()
	root.add_child(_roundtrip)
	await physics_frame
	_roundtrip.award_points(50)
	await physics_frame
	var loaded := _save.load_state()
	_check(int(loaded.get("points", -1)) == 50,
		"award_points(50) is on disk as points == 50",
		"(got %s)" % loaded.get("points"))
	_check(loaded.get("equipped_weapon_id") == "pistol",
		"and the persisted equipped weapon is correct",
		"(got %s)" % loaded.get("equipped_weapon_id"))


--- FILE: tests/interact.gd ---
extends SceneTree

## Interaction harness for VibeFPS (Phase 3, lobby track A): the ShopPoint and
## DoorPoint interactables, the player's nearest_interactable() reach pick, and
## the E (interact) route through the input gate.
##
## The SaveSystem autoload does not exist yet (a later task wires it into
## project.godot), so the harness builds one by hand at
## user://interact_test.json -- the same isolation tests/progression.gd uses, so
## the production user://save.json is never touched. Scene transitions go
## through an injected fake changer: headless has no window to swap scenes in,
## and the production scenes must not be loaded out from under the harness.
##
## The player is the shipped player.tscn instantiated on the root, with a probe
## script answering the gate with an explicit mouse mode -- headless cannot
## capture the cursor (Input.mouse_mode stays VISIBLE), exactly as
## tests/movement.gd does. Interact lives in player.gd's _unhandled_input, so it
## is driven with a parsed key event plus Input.flush_buffered_events():
## Input.action_press alone never reaches _unhandled_input (tests/movement.gd
## pins the same rule for jump), and an InputEventAction pressed while the
## action is already pressed is deduplicated before it reaches the tree -- which
## would make the second press in the gate check arrive nowhere. The key event
## is the honest shape of the input anyway: input_actions.gd pins interact to
## the physical E key.
##
## Run: godot --headless --path . --script res://tests/interact.gd
##      .\tools\run_checks.ps1 interact


## Stand-in for player.gd, used only by this harness.
class _PlayerProbe extends "res://player.gd":
	## Mouse mode the gate is evaluated against instead of Input.mouse_mode.
	var gate_mouse_mode: int = Input.MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED

	func _mouse_mode_for_play_input() -> int:
		return gate_mouse_mode


## Records what ShopPoint announces. The signal is the observable the gate check
## needs: a second "purchase" of an already-owned weapon only equips, so it
## changes no points and leaves the equipped id where it was -- without the
## signal a gated press and an ungated one would look identical.
class _ShopLog extends RefCounted:
	var ok_values: Array[bool] = []

	func _on_interacted(ok: bool) -> void:
		ok_values.append(ok)


## Isolated path every interaction uses. user://save.json is never touched.
const _TMP_PATH := "user://interact_test.json"
const _PLAYER_SCENE := "res://player.tscn"
const _SHOP_SCRIPT := "res://scripts/shop_point.gd"
const _DOOR_SCRIPT := "res://scripts/door_point.gd"

## The reach the design fixes, restated here so a silent retune to 2.5 -- the
## value the brief explicitly overrules (R2: the shop at 3.0 m must be found,
## the door at 6.0 m must not) -- cannot turn this harness into a check of a
## different number.
const _EXPECTED_RANGE := 3.5
## The shops's stand-in price is whatever GameState reads from auto.tres; the
## points the harness grants match it so the arithmetic below is exact.
const _AUTO_PRICE := 300

var _failures: PackedStringArray = []
var _checks := 0

var _save: SaveSystem
var _gs: GameState
var _shop: ShopPoint
var _door: DoorPoint
var _player: CharacterBody3D
var _shop_log: _ShopLog
## Paths handed to the fake scene changer, in call order. No scene is actually
## swapped -- the fake only records what it was asked for.
var _changed_to: PackedStringArray = []


func _check(ok: bool, label: String, detail: String = "") -> void:
	_checks += 1
	if ok:
		print("  [PASS] %s %s" % [label, detail])
	else:
		_failures.append("%s %s" % [label, detail])
		print("  [FAIL] %s %s" % [label, detail])


func _finish() -> void:
	print("=== RESULT: %d checks, %d failed ===" % [_checks, _failures.size()])
	for f in _failures:
		print("  FAILURE: %s" % f)


## Fake scene changer: records the requested path and does nothing else. A real
## change_scene_to_file would tear down this very SceneTree under the harness.
func _fake_changer(path: String) -> void:
	_changed_to.append(path)


## PackedStringArray vs Array comparison by contents. Godot does not treat the
## two container types as equal, so a plain `owned == ["pistol"]` would be false
## even when the contents match.
func _packed_eq(packed: PackedStringArray, expected: Array) -> bool:
	if packed.size() != expected.size():
		return false
	for i in packed.size():
		if packed[i] != expected[i]:
			return false
	return true


## Dispatchable key event bound to the interact action. The gate check uses this
## instead of InputEventAction on purpose: an InputEventAction pressed while the
## action is already pressed is deduplicated before it reaches the tree, so the
## second press in a row would silently arrive nowhere. A key event has no such
## dedup, and it is the honest shape of the input anyway -- input_actions.gd pins
## interact to the physical E key.
func _interact_key_event(pressed: bool) -> InputEventKey:
	var ev := InputEventKey.new()
	ev.physical_keycode = KEY_E
	ev.keycode = KEY_E
	ev.pressed = pressed
	return ev


## The hint string an @export carries ("Node3D", "WeaponData"...), a marker of an
## object-typed export. Plain String exports carry no hint, so the type check
## below reads the property's declared Variant.Type instead.
func _property_hint(node: Object, property_name: String) -> String:
	for entry in node.get_property_list():
		if String(entry.get("name", "")) == property_name:
			return String(entry.get("hint_string", ""))
	return "<missing>"


## The declared Variant.Type of an export, straight from get_property_list().
func _property_type(node: Object, property_name: String) -> Variant.Type:
	for entry in node.get_property_list():
		if String(entry.get("name", "")) == property_name:
			return entry.get("type", TYPE_NIL)
	return TYPE_NIL


func _cleanup() -> void:
	DirAccess.remove_absolute(ProjectSettings.globalize_path(_TMP_PATH))


## The engine registers GameState/SaveSystem as project autoloads
## (project.godot, Task 6) and installs nodes of those names under /root even in
## --script mode. Left in place they would shadow the harness's own nodes of the
## same name (Godot renames the later one, so /root/GameState would resolve to
## the autoload, which reads user://save.json at _ready and would restore points
## from an unrelated file) and ShopPoint/DoorPoint would talk to it instead.
## Drop them first, so the harness owns /root/GameState and /root/SaveSystem
## exactly as it did before the autoloads existed. Immediate free, not
## queue_free: the names must be free before the nodes below are added.
func _drop_autoloads() -> void:
	for autoload_name in ["GameState", "SaveSystem"]:
		var node := root.get_node_or_null(autoload_name)
		if node != null:
			root.remove_child(node)
			node.free()


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# world builders
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

## A floor under the player so the physics ticks the gate check awaits cannot
## quietly drop it out of the shop's reach. Top face at y = 0, where the
## player capsule's bottom sits (1.8 height centred at 0.9 in player.tscn).
func _new_floor() -> void:
	var floor_body := StaticBody3D.new()
	floor_body.name = "Floor"
	var shape := CollisionShape3D.new()
	var box := BoxShape3D.new()
	box.size = Vector3(40.0, 1.0, 40.0)
	shape.shape = box
	floor_body.add_child(shape)
	floor_body.position = Vector3(0.0, -0.5, 0.0)
	root.add_child(floor_body)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# entry point
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

func _initialize() -> void:
	print("=== VibeFPS interact test ===")
	_drop_autoloads()
	# Clean sheet: a leftover save from an interrupted run must not be restored
	# into the state under test.
	_cleanup()

	_save = SaveSystem.new()
	# The name is what makes /root/SaveSystem resolve for GameState's autosave.
	_save.name = "SaveSystem"
	_save.save_path = _TMP_PATH
	root.add_child(_save)

	_gs = GameState.new()
	# The name is what makes /root/GameState resolve for ShopPoint/DoorPoint.
	_gs.name = "GameState"
	# Injected before add_child, so GameState._ready() must not overwrite it.
	_gs.scene_changer = _fake_changer
	root.add_child(_gs)

	_new_floor()

	# The two interactables are built before the player, so the player's first
	# nearest_interactable() already sees both.
	_shop = ShopPoint.new()
	_shop.weapon_id = "auto"
	_shop.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0, -3.0)
	root.add_child(_shop)

	_door = DoorPoint.new()
	_door.location_id = "range"
	_door.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0, -6.0)
	root.add_child(_door)

	var packed: PackedScene = load(_PLAYER_SCENE)
	if packed == null:
		print("FATAL: cannot load %s" % _PLAYER_SCENE)
		_finish()
		quit(1)
		return
	_player = packed.instantiate() as CharacterBody3D
	if _player == null:
		print("FATAL: %s does not instantiate to a CharacterBody3D" % _PLAYER_SCENE)
		_finish()
		quit(1)
		return
	# Swapped before the node enters the tree, so @onready and _ready run on the
	# probe script. Everything under test is the real player.gd; only the mouse
	# mode the gate reads comes from the harness.
	_player.set_script(_PlayerProbe)
	_player.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
	root.add_child(_player)

	_shop_log = _ShopLog.new()
	_shop.interacted.connect(_shop_log._on_interacted)

	# Let every _ready run (group join, collision layers, GameState restore).
	await physics_frame

	_test_contracts()
	_test_shop_purchase()
	_test_door_transition()
	_test_nearest_interactable()
	await _test_input_gate()

	_save.clear_save()
	_cleanup()

	_finish()
	quit(1 if _failures.size() > 0 else 0)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# checks
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------

## The references later tasks bind to: a renamed class, export, signal or layer
## is a silent break for lobby.tscn and for the interaction itself.
func _test_contracts() -> void:
	print("-- contracts: the classes, exports, signals, layers and group")
	var shop_script := load(_SHOP_SCRIPT) as Script
	_check(shop_script != null, "shop_point.gd loads")
	if shop_script == null:
		return
	_check(shop_script.get_global_name() == "ShopPoint",
		"shop_point.gd declares class_name ShopPoint",
		"(got \"%s\")" % shop_script.get_global_name())
	_check(shop_script.get_instance_base_type() == "Area3D",
		"ShopPoint extends Area3D", "(got %s)" % shop_script.get_instance_base_type())
	_check(_shop.has_method("interact"), "ShopPoint exposes interact()")
	_check(_shop.has_signal("interacted"), "ShopPoint exposes the interacted signal")
	_check(_property_type(_shop, "weapon_id") == TYPE_STRING,
		"weapon_id is a String export", "(got type %s)" % str(_property_type(_shop, "weapon_id")))
	_check(int(_shop.collision_layer) == 1 << 5,
		"ShopPoint lives on layer 6 (interactable)", "(got %d)" % int(_shop.collision_layer))
	_check(int(_shop.collision_mask) == 1 << 1,
		"and its mask is the player layer", "(got %d)" % int(_shop.collision_mask))
	_check(_shop.monitoring, "ShopPoint monitors overlaps")
	_check(_shop.is_in_group("interactable"), "ShopPoint joins the interactable group")

	var door_script := load(_DOOR_SCRIPT) as Script
	_check(door_script != null, "door_point.gd loads")
	if door_script == null:
		return
	_check(door_script.get_global_name() == "DoorPoint",
		"door_point.gd declares class_name DoorPoint",
		"(got \"%s\")" % door_script.get_global_name())
	_check(door_script.get_instance_base_type() == "Area3D",
		"DoorPoint extends Area3D", "(got %s)" % door_script.get_instance_base_type())
	_check(_door.has_method("interact"), "DoorPoint exposes interact()")
	_check(_property_type(_door, "location_id") == TYPE_STRING,
		"location_id is a String export", "(got type %s)" % str(_property_type(_door, "location_id")))
	_check(int(_door.collision_layer) == 1 << 5,
		"DoorPoint lives on layer 6 (interactable)", "(got %d)" % int(_door.collision_layer))
	_check(int(_door.collision_mask) == 1 << 1,
		"and its mask is the player layer", "(got %d)" % int(_door.collision_mask))
	_check(_door.monitoring, "DoorPoint monitors overlaps")
	_check(_door.is_in_group("interactable"), "DoorPoint joins the interactable group")


func _test_shop_purchase() -> void:
	print("-- shop: refused without points, bought with them")
	_check(_gs.points == 0, "precondition: the player starts with no points",
		"(got %d)" % _gs.points)
	var refused := _shop.interact(_player)
	_check(not refused, "the shop refuses the auto without points")
	_check(_packed_eq(_gs.owned_weapons, ["pistol"]),
		"and does not hand it over", "(got %s)" % _gs.owned_weapons)
	_check(_gs.points == 0, "and charges nothing", "(got %d)" % _gs.points)
	_check(_shop_log.ok_values.size() == 1 and _shop_log.ok_values[0] == false,
		"the refused sale is announced as a refusal",
		"(got %s)" % str(_shop_log.ok_values))

	_gs.award_points(_AUTO_PRICE)
	var bought := _shop.interact(_player)
	_check(bought, "the shop sells the %d auto once the player can afford it" % _AUTO_PRICE)
	_check("auto" in _gs.owned_weapons, "the auto is now owned", "(got %s)" % _gs.owned_weapons)
	_check(_gs.equipped_weapon_id == "auto", "and equipped",
		"(got \"%s\")" % _gs.equipped_weapon_id)
	_check(_gs.points == 0, "the price was deducted", "(got %d)" % _gs.points)
	_check(_shop_log.ok_values.size() == 2 and _shop_log.ok_values[1] == true,
		"the sale is announced as a success", "(got %s)" % str(_shop_log.ok_values))


func _test_door_transition() -> void:
	print("-- door: entering the range goes through the scene changer")
	var entered := _door.interact(_player)
	_check(entered, "the door enters the unlocked range")
	_check(_changed_to.size() > 0 and _changed_to[_changed_to.size() - 1] == "res://range.tscn",
		"the changer was asked for res://range.tscn", "(got %s)" % _changed_to)


## The reach the brief overrules 2.5 with (R2): a shop at 3.0 m is inside 3.5 m
## and a door at 6.0 m is outside it, so a 2.5 m range or a 6.0 m one both fail
## this pair.
func _test_nearest_interactable() -> void:
	print("-- reach: the nearest interactable within %.1f m, and nothing beyond" % _EXPECTED_RANGE)
	_player.global_position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
	var nearest: Node = _player.call("nearest_interactable")
	_check(nearest == _shop,
		"the shop at 3.0 m is found (the range is 3.5, not 2.5)",
		"(got %s)" % ("null" if nearest == null else nearest.name))

	_shop.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0, -10.0)
	nearest = _player.call("nearest_interactable")
	_check(nearest == null,
		"with the shop at 10 m and the door at 6 m, nothing is in reach",
		"(got %s)" % ("null" if nearest == null else nearest.name))
	# Put the shop back: the gate check below needs a target an ungated press
	# can actually reach.
	_shop.position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0, -3.0)


## Interact is play input: it must not be a way around a free cursor. With the
## cursor free the same press has to do nothing at all, and capturing it has to
## be the only thing that changes.
func _test_input_gate() -> void:
	print("-- input gate: interact does not get around the cursor being free")
	_player.global_position = Vector3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
	var before := _shop_log.ok_values.size()

	_player.set("gate_mouse_mode", Input.MOUSE_MODE_VISIBLE)
	Input.parse_input_event(_interact_key_event(true))
	# Synchronous delivery, exactly as tests/movement.gd does for jump: Godot
	# otherwise accumulates parsed events and flushes them only on the next main
	# loop iteration, which would let the assertions race the delivery.
	Input.flush_buffered_events()
	await physics_frame
	_check(_shop_log.ok_values.size() == before,
		"a press with the cursor free reaches neither the shop nor its signal",
		"(got %d signals)" % (_shop_log.ok_values.size() - before))
	_check(_gs.points == 0, "and spends no points",
		"(got %d)" % _gs.points)
	_check(_gs.equipped_weapon_id == "auto",
		"and leaves the equipped weapon alone", "(got \"%s\")" % _gs.equipped_weapon_id)

	# A real press returns through a release: the second press is a new press,
	# not a repeat of the first one.
	_player.set("gate_mouse_mode", Input.MOUSE_MODE_CAPTURED)
	Input.parse_input_event(_interact_key_event(false))
	Input.flush_buffered_events()
	await physics_frame
	Input.parse_input_event(_interact_key_event(true))
	Input.flush_buffered_events()
	await physics_frame
	_check(_shop_log.ok_values.size() == before + 1,
		"the same press reaches the shop once the cursor is captured",
		"(got %d signals)" % (_shop_log.ok_values.size() - before))
	if _shop_log.ok_values.size() == before + 1:
		_check(_shop_log.ok_values[before] == true,
			"and reports the second purchase as a success")

	# The second purchase of an owned weapon is an equip, not a charge.
	_check(_gs.equipped_weapon_id == "auto",
		"the second purchase only equips: auto stays equipped",
		"(got \"%s\")" % _gs.equipped_weapon_id)
	_check(_gs.points == 0, "and it charged nothing",
		"(got %d)" % _gs.points)
